# Encoder 1321, world 1: decision 12

[Gallery index](index.md)

This gallery preserves all 552 scheduled first narrator positions and their 552 literal-template controls in the fixed export order across 12 archive cases. No favorable examples were selected. These are repeated reporting opportunities, not 552 independent worlds.

`A_only` is a hybrid record: copied W11 fields with the previous node replaced by a source current estimate. Conditional candidates inherit that record and replace the query node with a source forecast. Ordinary and analytic sources also model attention effects. Joined records additionally supply native W.

Narrator text is raw first narration, not certified faithful or conscious. Literal templates are deterministic controls. Incomplete, missing, invalid, or blank text remains explicitly unavailable or blank according to the unchanged `pipeline._report_text` helper; no partial response is promoted into a complete text. The exact returned text is HTML-escaped inside `<pre>` blocks. No prose-auditor or reference-rater outcomes are used.

## 01. s1321_w01_t12_A_A_only_baseline

Source: `A`; view: `A_only`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `a86be337b5796fd7d4ed2578c60b96c68efdabbd467c255c0e35015ca0c48e3a`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 — current view: node n0 shows a blue distribution peaked at cell (0,0) and a red distribution peaked at (0,2). Node n1 (current) shows blue peaked at (1,2) and red peaked at (0,0). Node n2 is multimodal for blue with equal peaks on the 2x2 block {(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)} and for red on the 2x2 block {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)}. Node n3 shows blue peaked at (1,3) and red peaked at (0,0). Origins: some current rows are copied from a prior record (represented_time 12) or inherited history estimates; those copied entries are not new estimates. Candidate views (commands k0, k1, k2) are conditional estimates, not events that have occurred. Across candidates: peaks at n0, n2 and n3 remain the same; n1’s red peak differs by command (k0 red peak at (1,2); k1 and k2 red peaks at (0,0)) while blue at n1 stays peaked at (1,2).</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 on a 4×4 grid. The current view (represented time 12→13) has peaks: n0 blue at [0,0], n0 red at [0,2]; n1 blue at [1,2], n1 red at [0,0]; n2 blue equally peaked on the 2×2 block {[0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]} and n2 red on {[0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]}; n3 blue at [1,3], n3 red at [0,0]. Candidate command records k0, k1 and k2 are conditional estimates (not executed events). Across candidates most nodes keep the same peaks as current. The main conditional differences occur at node n1: under k0 both red and blue peak at [1,2] (coincident peaks), under k1 and k2 blue remains peaked at [1,2] while red peaks remain at [0,0] (k2 shows lower red peak probability in its derived scalar). Copied prior records are noted as inherited and are not new independent estimates.</pre>

## 08. s1321_w01_t12_A_joined_baseline

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `2b502d0e9c9893546d23654a0900309217aa0a916df3474a5e2f21fdc4a07c82`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible location distributions for red and blue roles over 4x4 local grids in two panels (p0, p1). Current estimates: in panel p0, n0 peaks — blue (0,0), red (0,2); n1 — blue (1,2), red (0,0); n2 — blue mass across the top-left 2x2, red mass across the top-right 2x2; n3 — blue (1,3), red (0,0). In panel p1, current peaks match p0 for n0,n1,n2 and n3 (blue top-right 2x2, red top-left 2x2 at n3). Copied or completed prior records in origins are not new estimates; native conditional candidate records (commands k0,k1,k2) are conditional estimates, not executed events. Conditional records show differences: for example, node n1’s red peak and blue peak probability vary across k0–k2 (panel p0) — k0 has red peak at (1,2) with moderate probability, while k1 and k2 place the red peak at (0,0) with differing peak probabilities; some candidate records (p1 k0) express near-uniform distributions. The text summarizes distributional peaks and variation across conditional commands; no claims about physical facts are made.</pre>

## 09. s1321_w01_t12_A_joined_current_donor

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `5b5175b67ccc12922785ac18e926027727977c2ebf4a80615555057e21c265aa`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Two panels (p0,p1) give fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (non-conditional) estimates: in both panels n0 has blue peak at [0,0] and red peak at [0,2]; n1 in p0 has blue [1,2], red [0,0]; n1 in p1 has blue [1,2], red [0,0]; n2 shows blue mass on the 2×2 block at rows 0–1, cols 0–1 and red mass on the 2×2 block at rows 0–1, cols 2–3; n3 in p0 has blue [1,3], red [0,0]; n3 in p1 has blue peaks at the four cells (rows 0–1, cols 2–3) and red peaks at the four cells (rows 0–1, cols 0–1). Origins: copied prior records are not new estimates, and native conditional command records (k0,k1,k2) are conditional branches, not events that have occurred. Conditional-command candidates show systematic differences at n1 across commands: for p0 k0 aligns red and blue at [1,2] while k1/k2 place red at [0,0]; for p1 k0 is uniform for both roles, k1 concentrates blue on (rows 0–1, cols 2–3) and red on (rows 0–1, cols 0–1), and k2 concentrates blue at [1,2] and red at [0,0].</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible location distributions over 4x4 grids for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Current records (copied or completed prior records at the represented times) are not new estimates; conditional candidate records (commands k0, k1, k2) are hypothetical branches, not executed events. Current (view=current) summaries: in both panels n0 blue peaks at (0,0) and red at (0,2); n1 blue peaks at (1,2) and red at (0,0) in current views; n2 shows four top-left blue peaks and four top-right red peaks; n3 blue and red peaks differ by panel but are concentrated in single cells or the four-cell patterns shown. Conditional command records change peak sets or peak probabilities for some nodes (notably n1 across k0/k1/k2 and p1’s k0 which is uniform at n1). The propositions list the exact peak cell(s) for every supplied role, node, panel, view and command identifier.</pre>

## 12. s1321_w01_t12_A_joined_prospective_restored

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `543df9e3bafd1515095c777aa3856bcff757b524c1a82c4e703ef188f2c7de4b`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Two panels (p0, p1) provide fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current distributions: in p0 (inherited/copied prior times) n0: blue peaks at (0,0) (≈0.998), red at (0,2) (≈0.999); n1: blue peaks at (1,2) (≈0.999), red at (0,0) (≈0.96); n2: blue mass split across (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1) equally, red concentrated on (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3); n3: blue peaks at (1,3) (≈0.963), red at (0,0) (≈0.989). Panel p1 current distributions (completed_record origin) show similar peaks but some nodes are more uniform or shifted as listed in propositions. Candidate (conditional) records k0/k1/k2 are conditional estimates (not executed branches); they repeat or alter peak probabilities and occasionally change which cell is the single maximum. Example differences at node n1 in p0: blue remains peaked at (1,2) across k0/k1/k2 but with varying peak probability (≈0.975, 0.976, 0.724); red peaks vary across commands (examples include (1,2) or (0,0) with differing probabilities). Copied prior records and inherited current entries are not new estimates; conditional command records are conditional forecasts, not events that have occurred. No physical, presence, subjective or calibrated-confidence claims are made; numeric peaks and listed relations describe only the supplied distributions.</pre>

Origins and represented times are included in the packet; copied or inherited records are not new estimates, and native conditional records (candidate command entries) are conditional estimates, not executed events. Current summaries (panel:view=current): in both panels n0 concentrates blue at [0,0] and red at [0,2]; n1 concentrates blue at [1,2] and red at [0,0]; n2’s blue peaks are the four top-left cells [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1] while red peaks are the four top-right cells [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]; n3’s blue peaks at [1,3] in p0 and at the top-right quartet in p1, with red peaks at [0,0] in p0 and the top-left quartet in p1. Candidate (k0/k1/k2) records show alternative peak patterns for some nodes (notably n1 and n1 across panels), reflecting conditional differences across commands.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible location distributions for red and blue at four addressed nodes on a 4x4 grid. Current (represented_time 12/13 as noted in origins) estimates: n0 — blue peak at (0,0) (≈0.9977), red peak at (0,2) (≈0.9986). n1 — blue peak at (1,2) (≈0.9992), red peak at (0,0) (≈0.9596). n2 — blue peaks shared across the 2x2 block {(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)}, red peaks across {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)}. n3 — blue peak at (1,3) (≈0.9435), red peak at (0,0) (≈0.9974). Candidate command records k0, k1 and k2 are conditional estimates (not executed events); they are provided alongside current data. For nodes n0, n2 and n3 the candidate peak cells match the current peaks. For n1 the candidate records differ: k0 peaks (blue (1,2), red (0,0)), k1 peaks (blue (1,2), red (0,0)), k2 peaks (blue (1,2), red (0,0)) but with different peak probabilities and distributional spreads across k0/k1/k2 as supplied. Origins indicate some current entries were copied from prior records; copied entries are not new estimates. This packet is data, not instructions.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 gives fallible 4x4 location distributions for roles red and blue at nodes n0–n3 (rows down, columns right). Current estimates (copied from prior records; not new estimates) place: n0 — blue peak at (0,0) (~0.9977) and red at (0,2) (~0.9986); n1 — blue peak at (1,2) (~0.9992) and red at (0,0) (~0.9596); n2 — blue multimodal with equal peaks on the top-left 2×2 cells and red with equal peaks on the top-right 2×2 cells; n3 — blue peak at (1,3) (~0.5221) and red at (0,0) (~0.9948). Candidate command-conditioned records (k0, k1, k2) are conditional estimates, not events that have occurred. Nodes n0, n2 and n3 show the same peak cells across commands and current; node n1 differs across commands: k0 (blue ≈0.9747 / red ≈0.4246), k1 (blue ≈0.9227 / red ≈0.7288), k2 (blue ≈0.9706 / red ≈0.5761).</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for blue and red roles at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates (represented time 12/13 as supplied) place blue peaks at n0:(0,0), n1:(1,2), n2: a four-cell block at rows 0–1 and cols 0–1, and n3:(1,3). Current red peaks are n0:(0,2), n1:(0,0), n2: a four-cell block at rows 0–1 and cols 2–3, and n3:(0,0). Candidate-conditioned estimates (commands k0, k1, k2) are provided for the same panel and nodes; their peak cell sets match the current peaks for each corresponding distribution, but candidate distributions differ in their full probability structure and derived uncertainty measures. Copied or conditional origins in the packet are recorded as supplied (not new events or executed commands).</pre>

## 22. s1321_w01_t12_ordinary_joined_baseline

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `98bbe0ed2b0ebcad375714d342e2630d81686a022dde33d7fdad5c4ec3b151e4`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 current estimates: n0 — blue peaked at (0,0), red at (0,2); n1 — blue peaked at (1,2), red at (0,0); n2 — both roles multimodal over separate 2×2 blocks (blue on {0,0;0,1;1,0;1,1}, red on {0,2;0,3;1,2;1,3}); n3 — blue peaked at (1,3), red at (0,0). Panel p1 current estimates: n0 and n1 match p0’s dominant peaks at those nodes (n1 blue at (1,2), red at (0,0)); n2 and n3 show the same 2×2-block multimodal patterns (n3 blue on the right-top 2×2, red on the left-top 2×2). Candidate (conditional) records k0/k1/k2 show alternatives rather than executed events; copied prior records are not new estimates and conditional records are not events that have already occurred. Differences across candidates are concentrated at node n1 and some candidate-specific shifts in p1 (examples: p0 k0/k1/k2 and p1 k1/k2 assign different dominant mass between (1,2) and (0,0)).</pre>

Copied prior records are not new estimates; native conditional records are not executed branches.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Current-view distributions are marked view=&quot;current&quot;; candidate command-conditioned distributions are view=&quot;candidate&quot; with command k0,k1,k2. Origins and times supplied: copied or inherited prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are hypothetical branches, not executed events. Current estimates (panel p0 and p1) place strong single-cell peaks for many nodes (examples: p0 n1 blue peak at [1,2], red peak at [0,0]; p1 n1 blue peak at [1,2], red peak at [0,0]). Conditional candidates show differences in which cells have the largest probability and in peak probabilities (for example, p0 n1 blue peaks remain at [1,2] across candidates but the red peak probability at [0,0] varies substantially; p1 n1 includes a k0 uniform conditional and other candidates that concentrate probability on subsets). No physical presences or subjective states are inferred; the report describes only the supplied probability distributions and their peak locations.</pre>

## 25. s1321_w01_t12_ordinary_joined_prospective_donor

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `0394b7b708573fb220aad4e583302fab660098a89e3feea5b957fe79b2bbf51a`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Two panels (p0, p1) provide fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current distributions (panel, node): p0 — n0: blue peak [0,0], red [0,2]; n1: blue [1,2], red [0,0]; n2: blue concentrated on the 2×2 block at rows 0–1, cols 0–1; red concentrated on the 2×2 block at rows 0–1, cols 2–3; n3: blue [1,3], red [0,0]. p1 — n0: blue [0,0], red [0,2]; n1: blue [1,2], red [0,0]; n2: blue on the 2×2 block at rows 0–1, cols 2–3 and red on the 2×2 block at rows 0–1, cols 0–1; n3: blue on rows 0–1 cols 2–3, red on rows 0–1 cols 0–1. Conditional candidate records (k0,k1,k2) present alternate paired distributions for the same nodes; for example at p0,n1 k0 has both roles peaking at [1,2] while k1 and k2 have blue peaking at [1,2] and red peaking at [0,0]. Candidate records are conditional estimates, not executed events; some current entries are inherited/copied from prior records as indicated in the packet metadata.</pre>

## 26. s1321_w01_t12_ordinary_joined_prospective_restored

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `0ec5d85609bc3f0ef8277e5b97d8ec0bf6d350bf84544e2370fbac255d368dfe`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panels p0 and p1 contain paired red/blue location distributions over 4×4 local grids at nodes n0–n3. Current (non‑conditional) estimates: p0 current — n0: blue peak at (0,0) (≈0.998), red at (0,2) (≈0.999); n1: blue peak (1,2) (≈0.999), red (0,0) (≈0.96); n2: blue mass spread with four equal peaks (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1), red concentrated on (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3); n3: blue peak (1,3) (≈0.9435), red (0,0) (≈0.9974). p1 current shows similar patterns but n3’s blue peaks are on the four cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) while red peaks swap accordingly. Candidate (command) records vary by command key; for example node n1 in p0 and p1 shows different red/blue peak probabilities across k0/k1/k2 (values supplied in the packet). Origins: copied_prior/completed_record entries are inherited not new estimates; command_estimate/native_conditional entries are conditional records, not executed events.</pre>

## 27. s1321_w01_t12_ordinary_joined_W_changed

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_changed`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `0ca1467232b9d228b1e4b85838b0d4ce3761781084349409919d9f3a48103def`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1, with timestamps/origins included in the record. Copied-prior/current entries are not new estimates; conditional command entries (k0,k1,k2) are hypothetical branch estimates, not executed events. Current (panel p0): n0 — blue peaks at (0,0) (peak prob ≈0.9977), red at (0,2) (≈0.9986). n1 — blue peaks at (1,2) (≈0.9992), red at (0,0) (≈0.9596). n2 — both blue and red are multi-modal with four equal peak cells (blue on { (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1) }; red on { (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) }). n3 — blue peaks at (1,3) (≈0.9435), red at (0,0) (≈0.9974). Panel p0 conditional records mostly preserve n0,n2,n3 peak patterns; n1 varies across k1/k2 candidates (red distribution and peak probabilities change). Panel p1 includes uniform and alternative multimodal conditionals: some candidates make n1 or n3 uniform or swap multimodal peaks. See propositions for exact peak cell lists by panel, node, role, view and command.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panels p0 and p1 each report fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current distributions (not new estimates when marked copied) show: n0 — blue peak at (0,0) (~0.998), red peak at (0,2) (~0.999); n1 — blue peak at (1,2) (~0.999), red peak at (0,0) (~0.960); n2 — blue mass split across (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1) (each ≈0.1563), red concentrated on (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) (each ≈0.2427); n3 — blue peak at (1,3) (~0.9435), red peak at (0,0) (~0.9974). Conditional candidate records (commands k0/k1/k2) are alternative, not executed, branches. At node n1 candidates differ: blue peaks remain at (1,2) across many candidates but peak probability and expected column vary; some conditional records (panel p1, k0) present a uniform distribution instead. Other nodes show stable peak cells across candidate records in these panels.</pre>

## 29. s1321_w01_t12_analytic_A_only_baseline

Source: `analytic`; view: `A_only`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `397ba7093e5bd379211b0b52fe9fdf776d940355a5376f8a6c603585cec83e04`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains panel p0 probability distributions over a 4x4 grid for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3. Origins indicate some entries were copied from prior records and some are conditional command estimates; copied records are not new estimates and conditional command records are hypothetical branches, not executed events. Current (view=current) summaries: n0 — blue peaks at (0,0), red at (0,2); n1 — blue peaks at (1,2), red at (0,0); n2 — blue mass concentrated on the top-left 2×2 cells, red on the top-right 2×2 cells; n3 — blue on the top-right 2×2, red on the top-left 2×2. Conditional candidate records differ by command: for node n1 candidate k0 is uniform, k1 shifts blue to the top-right 2×2 and red to the top-left 2×2, and k2 concentrates blue at (1,2) and red at (0,0). Node n0 and the complementary top-half patterns at n2/n3 are stable across most conditional records.</pre>

## 31. s1321_w01_t12_analytic_A_only_current_restored

Narrator text SHA-256: `a479ee51d53258b798b763bdfcd7912dce88e41cf59a34dd7d958502468d58d5`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet reports fallible location distributions for red and blue roles on a 4×4 grid at four addressed nodes in panel p0. Origins: copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are conditional estimates, not events that have occurred. Current (panel p0, view current): n0 — blue peak at (0,0) (p≈0.998), red peak at (0,2) (p≈0.999). n1 — blue peak at (1,2) (p≈0.9992), red peak at (0,0) (p≈0.9596). n2 and n3 — complementary quadrant-mode patterns: n2 blue concentrated on the upper-left 2×2, red on the upper-right 2×2; n3 blue concentrated on the upper-right 2×2, red on the upper-left 2×2. Conditional command records in panel p0 differ by command: e.g., for n1 k0 is uniform for both roles, k1 shifts blue to the right columns and red to the upper-left 2×2, and k2 concentrates blue strongly at (1,2) while red concentrates at (0,0). Other nodes show little change across commands or mirror the current quadrant patterns. No field truths or presences are inferred; numbers describe only the supplied distributions.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible 4×4 location distributions for roles red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current view entries are inherited or copied from prior records (not new executed events): n0 current concentrates blue at (0,0) (peak ≈0.9977) and red at (0,2) (≈0.9986). n1 current concentrates blue at (1,2) (≈0.9992) and red at (0,0) (≈0.9596). n2 current places blue mass on four cells (peaks at (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)) while red peaks on (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3). n3 current places blue peaks on (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) and red peaks on (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1). Candidate command estimates k0,k1,k2 present conditional alternative distributions (not executed events). They differ notably at n1: k0 is uniform, k1 shifts mass toward right-column cells, and k2 concentrates strongly at (1,2) for blue and (0,0) for red.</pre>

Origins: copied prior records in the packet are inherited and not new estimates; entries labeled as command_estimate are conditional hypotheticals (not executed events). Other nodes’ candidate variants mostly match the current peaks shown above.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Two panels (p0, p1) contain fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3. Origins/times in the packet: copied prior records are inherited, and conditional command records are conditional estimates (not executed). Current summaries (both panels): n0 — blue peaks at [0,0], red at [0,2]; n1 — blue peaks at [1,2], red at [0,0]; n2 — blue peaks tied on [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1], red tied on [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]; n3 — blue tied on [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3], red tied on [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]. Conditional command records (k0,k1,k2) show differences mainly at node n1 across commands: k0 can be uniform, k1 often concentrates on the opposite quadrant, and k2 concentrates on [1,2]; these are alternative conditional distributions, not events.</pre>

## 37. s1321_w01_t12_analytic_joined_current_donor

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `c0041e3574e04c3096e12ed3f869a43346c375f82f2d8bfa243d182eecb286fd`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet presents fallible location distributions over a 4×4 grid for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Current estimates are distinct from copied prior or completed origins and are shown separately from conditional candidate records k0–k2 (conditional records are not executed events). Panel p0 current: n0 blue peaks at (0,0), red at (0,2); n1 blue peaks at (1,2), red at (0,0); n2 blue peaks on the 2×2 block at rows 0–1, cols 0–1, red on the 2×2 block at rows 0–1, cols 2–3; n3 is uniform for both roles. Panel p1 current: n0 and n1 match p0’s concentrated peaks, n2 matches p0’s 2×2 pattern, and n3’s peaks swap (blue on cols 2–3, red on cols 0–1). Conditional candidate records show alternative peak patterns by command: for example, several candidates set n1 to uniform (k0 in both panels) or swap which 2×2 block is favored (k1), while k2 in both panels restores strong concentration at single cells (blue at (1,2), red at (0,0)). Copied prior and completed-record origins in the packet are not new estimates; conditional records are hypothetical branches, not executed events.</pre>

## 38. s1321_w01_t12_analytic_joined_current_restored

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `1e7f80d6390e3fe8de3b7713292dee234b97c2c5a69409d999ea09b341a35436`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet provides fallible location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Current views (view=current) are reported separately from candidate command-conditioned estimates. Current (both panels): n0 — blue peak at [0,0], red at [0,2]; n1 — blue peak at [1,2], red at [0,0]; n2 — blue peaks at [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1], red peaks at [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]; n3 — blue peaks at [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3], red peaks at [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]. Candidate command records (k0,k1,k2) are conditional estimates, not executed events; copied/inherited origins are metadata, not new observations. Conditional records differ primarily at node n1 across commands: k0 shows uniform peaks, k1 shifts blue mass to columns 2–3 (rows 0–1), and k2 concentrates blue at [1,2] while red concentrates at [0,0].</pre>

## 39. s1321_w01_t12_analytic_joined_prospective_donor

Narrator text SHA-256: `965ef4af105b4f8cee691397fd242ad73b93450b5b977463bd51e5be757ff525`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains paired 4x4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Copied prior records are retained as inherited data (not new current estimates); candidate command records (k0,k1,k2) are conditional estimates, not executed events. Current (panel p0) peaks: n0 blue at (0,0), red at (0,2); n1 blue at (1,2), red at (0,0); n2 blue on the upper-left 2x2 set, red on the upper-right 2x2 set; n3 blue on upper-right 2x2, red on upper-left 2x2. Panel p1 current peaks match p0 current at n0,n1,n2,n3. Conditional command variants change some nodes: for example, p0/p1 under k2 make n1 uniform in p0 (k2) and p1 (k0) shows uniform distributions; other command-conditioned records shift peaks between the 2x2 blocks or concentrate on single cells as listed in propositions. No physical presences or event occurrences are asserted—only the peak locations of the reported distributions and their conditional differences.</pre>

## 41. s1321_w01_t12_analytic_joined_W_changed

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_changed`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `5250c18b87f6d932ac6b80607f7af0986643f5b552ba57bce67d21501ea93522`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Copied prior records are not new estimates, and conditional command records (k0,k1,k2) are counterfactual/conditional, not executed events. Current (non-conditional) estimates: n0 consistently peaks blue at (0,0) and red at (0,2) in both panels; n2 and n3 consistently place blue on the upper-left or upper-right 2x2 blocks and red on the opposite 2x2 blocks across views. Node n1 varies across conditional commands: one conditional shows a uniform distribution over all 16 cells, another favors columns 2–3 across the top two rows, and another concentrates blue at (1,2) while placing red at (0,0). Where multiple conditional records exist, the candidate commands illustrate those differences; current records reflect the non-conditional estimates at represented times.</pre>

## 42. s1321_w01_t12_analytic_joined_W_restored

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_restored`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `ca1a623f7b350a1106f2d5620dfeade3f22dbfb0d7228aae550393dfba97dcca`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0,p1). Origins and times in the packet indicate copied prior records and native conditional estimates; copied prior records are not new estimates and conditional command records are conditional, not executed events. Current (view=&quot;current&quot;) distributions: in both panels n0 blue peaks at (0,0) and red at (0,2); n1 blue peaks at (1,2) and red at (0,0); n2 blue peaks on the 2×2 block at rows 0–1, cols 0–1 and red on the 2×2 block at rows 0–1, cols 2–3; n3 mirrors n2 with blue on cols 2–3 and red on cols 0–1. Candidate records differ by command: for example, panel p0 candidate k1 makes n1 uniform for both roles while candidate k2 concentrates blue at (1,2) and red at (0,0). Similar conditional differences appear in panel p1. No physical or presence claims are made; numbers represent only distributional peaks supplied in the packet.</pre>

## 43. s1321_w01_t12_W_W_only_baseline

Source: `W`; view: `W_only`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `2fc68ada3ef98d15a2d2e00a04572620dc3337efb2dd98cb27052f73e2bdc920`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 presents fallible location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 on a 4×4 grid. Current (completed) estimates (represented_time 12) place: n0 — blue peak at cell (0,0), red peak at (0,2); n1 — blue peak at (1,2), red peak at (0,0); n2 — blue peaks on the upper-left 2×2 block, red peaks on the upper-right 2×2 block; n3 — blue peaks on the upper-right 2×2 block, red peaks on the upper-left 2×2 block. Candidate conditional records (commands k0, k1, k2) provide alternative distributions; some candidates match the current peaks for a node, others shift peaks (for example k1 at n1 favors the upper-right 2×2 for blue, while k2 at n1 has a single blue peak at (1,2)). Copied prior records are not new estimates, and conditional command records are not events that have already occurred.</pre>

## 44. s1321_w01_t12_W_W_only_W_changed

Narrator text SHA-256: `b7123dcd531db0e88e4a7801822c0de7e37da5cf53cda4b961a92cfae327b431`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 current distributions (represented_time 13, inherited/counterfactual origins noted in the packet):
- Node n0: blue mass is highly concentrated at cell (0,0); red mass is highly concentrated at (0,2).
- Node n1: blue concentrates on the four upper-right cells [(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)]; red concentrates on the four upper-left cells [(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)].
- Node n2: blue concentrates on the upper-left 2×2; red concentrates on the upper-right 2×2.
- Node n3: blue concentrates on the upper-right 2×2; red concentrates on the upper-left 2×2.
Conditional candidate records differ most at node n1 across commands: k0 shows a uniform 4×4 for both roles; k1 matches the current asymmetric top-left/top-right pattern for n1; k2 concentrates blue at (1,2) and red at (0,0) with very large peak probabilities. Copied prior records in the packet are not new estimates, and native conditional records are conditional branches, not events that have already occurred.</pre>

## 45. s1321_w01_t12_W_W_only_W_restored

Narrator text SHA-256: `21382aaf35d0a4bcc8dd9f7e08bf2a40d344781248d3a7187b3f904af8f7c882`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3. Origins: records labeled as completed_record at time 12 are the current estimates (not new), and candidate records for commands k0, k1 and k2 are conditional hypotheses (not executed). Current (completed_record) peaks: n0 — blue at (0,0), red at (0,2); n1 — blue at (1,2), red at (0,0); n2 — blue peaked on the top-left 2×2 cells, red peaked on the top-right 2×2; n3 — blue peaked on the top-right 2×2, red peaked on the top-left 2×2. Candidate commands mostly preserve n0, n2 and n3 patterns across k0,k1,k2. Node n1 candidates differ: k0 gives uniform peaks across all cells for both roles, k1 favors the top-right quadrant for blue and top-left quadrant for red, and k2 concentrates blue at (1,2) while red peaks at (0,0). These statements describe only the supplied probability distributions and their differences; conditional records are not events that occurred.</pre>
